```
"""
Step-function optimizer...
Uses analytic family f(x)=A x^{-1/2}+B(1-x)^gamma, plus local refinements.
"""
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(n): ...
def _fft_square(a): ...
def _score(a): ...
def _make_density(n, A, gamma, reverse=False, eps=0.5):
    # midpoint quadrature of f; endpoints adjust
    x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    f = np.empty(n)
    # avoid singular: x[0]=0.5/n already
    f = A / np.sqrt(x) + B * (1 - x)**gamma
    if reverse: f = f[::-1]
    f = np.maximum(f,0)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0: uniform
    p=f/s
    return p
```

Simplified local optimizer:
```
def _optimize_max(a0, tlimit, rng, verbose=False, label=''):
    a=np.asarray(a0,float).copy(); a=np.nan_to_num...; a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<.01 uniform; a/=s; a=a/max? no.
    n=len(a)
    b=None; score=np.inf; best=a; best_score=np.inf
    eta=0.2; eta_min=0.01; eta_max=10
    no_imp=0; max_no=300; resets=0; max_resets=20
    t0=time.monotonic()
    while time...:
       if b is None: b=_fft_square(a); score,_,maxb=_internal_score(a,b)
       if score<best_score: update
       if no_imp>max_no: reset by mixing with fresh param or noise; continue
       # active top: choose K between max(50, n//1000) and maybe 5000
       K=int(max(20, min(5000, n//200)))
       # Use top K bins, weights proportional to (b - (maxb - margin))? If just top K, discontinuous.
       order=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]  # returns largest? Need care.
       w=np.zeros_like(b); w[order]=1.0
       # maybe scale by (b/maxb) to emphasize maximum
       w[order] = 0.2 + 0.8*(b[order]/maxb)
       # normalize weights sum1? For subgradient of normalized weighted sum, constants don't matter.
       g=_fft_grad(w,a)
       # remove mean
       g -= np.mean(g)
       # line search over eta candidates around current
       best_trial=None; best_trial_score=np.inf; best_eta=eta
       for mult in (0.25,0.5,1,2,4):
           e=eta*mult
           with err: a_new=a*np.exp(-e*g)
           a_new=np.maximum(a_new,1e-300); ss=a_new.sum(); if ss<=... continue
           a_new /= ss
           b_new=_fft_square(a_new); sc,_,_=_internal_score(a_new,b_new)
           # also maybe if sc < score - ...
           if sc<best_trial_score: store
       if trial improved score or best_score: accept, eta=min(eta*1.2,10)
       else: eta=max(eta*0.7,0.01); if eta<0.05 perturb
       no_imp update
```
But active top K with K=n//200 for 8192=40. Too few? Active max bins maybe many near peak. Use K=max(200, n//32) ~256; for 8192 ~256. Or 10% of length? We can choose K=int(n*0.02)=164. Use K=256. Weighting among top bins. Subgradient of max uses all bins at max; top K approximates neighborhood. For smooth convergence, use softmax weights with focus high as previous? Need implement `_fft_square` robust: Use real FFT. For nonnegative a, square (fa*fa) not conjugate? Convolution a*a = ifft(fft(a)*fft(a)) because same sequence. Yes. Use `fa * fa` not conj. For derivative of weighted sum w*b, gradient with respect to a is 2 * ifft(fft(w) * fa)? Let's derive: b=c(a) with index. c_k=Σ_i a_i a_{k-i}. ∂/∂a_j = Σ_k w_k (a_{k-j}+a? If convolution full linear. The gradient = 2 * convolve(w, a) padded? Previous `_fft_grad(weights,a)` returns `2.0 * ifft(FFT(weights)* conj? or fa?)`. In code given: `c = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), n=N)[:n]` Wait that computes correlation, not convolution gradient? Let's inspect previous `_fft_grad(weights, a)`:
```
def _fft_grad(weights, a):
    n=int(a.size); L=weights.size; N=_pow2(L+n-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(weights,n=N)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), n=N)[:n]
    return 2.0*c
```

Param function:
```
def _make_param(n, alpha, beta, gamma, reverse=False):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    base=x**(-alpha)  # singular
    if beta==0: f=base
    else:
       if gamma==1: smooth=(1-x)
       else: smooth=(1-x)**gamma
       f=base + beta*smooth
       # optional add uniform? We can add gamma? add `delta` uniform
    if reverse: f=f[::-1]
    f=np.maximum(f,0)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0: uniform
    p=f/s
    return p
```

Detailed helper functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(x): ...
def _fft_square(a):
    n=int(a.size); L=2*n-1; N=_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=N).real[:L]
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    return b
```
For gradient, need weights length L. Use N=_pow2(L+n-1) = _pow2(3n-2)? Actually correlation length L+n-1=3n-2. For n=8192 N=262144? 3n-2=24574, pow2=32768? Wait pow2 of 24574 is 32768, okay. For square N=2n-1 pow2=16384. Gradient N maybe 32768. Fine.
```
def _grad_weighted(w,a):
    n=int(a.size); L=int(w.size); N=_pow2(L+n-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,n=N); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fw)*fa,n=N)[:n]
    return 2.0*c
```
Check with small example manually? We trust.

Internal score:
```
def _internal_score(a,b=None):
    if b is None: b=_fft_square(a)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-300: return inf,b,s
    maxb=float(b.max())
    score=2.0*a.size*maxb/(s*s)
    return score,b,maxb
```
If b from FFT has tiny negative clipped. Good.

Weights:
```
def _softmax_weights(b, focus, eps=1e-4):
   L=b.size; maxb=float(b.max())
   if maxb<=1e-300: return np.full(L,1/L),maxb
   z=(maxb-b)/maxb
   with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'): p=np.exp(-z*float(focus))
   s=p.sum()
   if not finite or s<1e-12:
       p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1
   else: p=(1-eps)*(p/s)+eps/L
   return p,maxb
```
Top weights:
```
def _top_weights(b,K):
   L=b.size; maxb=max
   K=min(max(1,int(K)),L)
   w=np.zeros(L)
   idx=np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:] maybe if K==L all
   w[idx]=0.1+0.9*(b[idx]/maxb if maxb>0 else 1)
   s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
   return w,maxb
```

Param generator:
```
def _make_density(n, alpha=0.5, beta=7.0, gamma=1.0, reverse=False, uniform=False):
    n=int(n)
    if n<1: n=1
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    if uniform:
       f=np.ones(n)
    else:
       # left singular base. If alpha <0? no
       base=np.power(x, -alpha) if alpha!=0 else np.ones(n)
       if beta<=1e-15:
           f=base
       else:
           if gamma==1.0: smooth=1.0-x
           else: smooth=np.power(1.0-x, gamma)
           f=base+float(beta)*smooth
       f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
    if reverse: f=f[::-1]
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: f=np.ones(n); s=n
    return f/s
```
For gamma<1, (1-x)^gamma at last x=1-0.5/n small positive. Good. For alpha=0.5, x[0]^(-0.5) finite. Add maybe `x[0]=1e-8` not needed.

But for alpha closer to0.25, base less singular. For alpha negative? no.

We could include a pure smooth with no singular: `alpha=1.0?` base=1/x? no. For pure smooth, use alpha=0? base=1 uniform. Better: separate `pure_smooth`:
```
def _make_pure_power(n, gamma): p=(1-x)^gamma normalized
```
But generic with beta large and base small? If alpha=0, base=1 uniform, plus beta smooth. For pure smooth, we can set beta large, e.g., 1e6, f≈smooth; but overflow? beta*smooth <=1e6, okay. Simpler param grid include `beta=np.inf`? In Python, if beta infinite, f inf where smooth>0. Avoid. Use separate.
We'll add `pure` flag in candidate list.

Let's write `_clean(a, target_sum=None)`:
```
def _clean_sequence(a, fallback=None):
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<0.01:
      if fallback is not None: return _clean_sequence(fallback)
      n=max(len(arr),1024)
      return _fallback_sequence(n)
   return arr / s
```

Let's ensure `_grad_weighted` with `np.conj(fw)*fa` when w length L and a length n, output length N. For real, `irfft` returns real. But due to FFT conventions, the correlation lags might be shifted? Need verify with small example via mental DFT? Let's test using convolution formula: N large, zero-pad x=w [M], y=a [n]. Inverse of conj(X)*Y: c_k = Σ_m X? Using orthonormal DFT: c_k = (1/N) Σ_j conj(X_j)Y_j e^{i...} = Σ_m conj? Actually standard theorem: if X=FT(x), Y=FT(y), then ifft(conj(X)*Y) = circular cross-correlation with x and y: r[k] = Σ_n conj(x[n]) y[(k+n) mod N]? Let's derive: DFT pair: Y_k=Σ_n y_n e^{-i2πkn/N}. conj(X)_l=Σ_m conj(x_m) e^{+i2πml/N}. Product sum: c_p=(1/N)Σ_l conj(X_l)Y_l e^{i2πlp/N} = Σ_{m,n} conj(x_m)y_n δ_{m-n+p?} = Σ_n y_n conj(x_{n-p?})? Let's solve: conj(X_l)=Σ_m x_m e^{i2πml/N} (real). Product: Σ_mn x_m y_n e^{i2π(l+m? wait conj X no negative: e^{+i2πml/N}) e^{-i2πnl/N} = e^{-i2π(n-m)l/N}. Inverse e^{+i2πpl/N}. Sum over l gives δ_{p - (n-m) mod N}. c_p=Σ_{n=m+p} x_m y_{m+p}=Σ_m x_m y_{m+p}. That's correlation with x at m, y at m+p. Here x=w, y=a. So c_p=Σ_m w_m a_{m+p}. For p=0:Σ w_m a_m; p=1:Σ w_m a_{m+1}. Our desired grad_j=Σ_m w_{j+m} a_m =Σ_m w_m a_{m-j}? Let p=-j. So desired is c_{-j}. The output c_p gives positive lags. We need reverse? For j, swap x,y: ifft(conj(Y)*X) gives Σ_m y_m x_{m+p}=Σ_m a_m w_{m+p}. We want a_m w_{m-j} => p=-j. We can take c_{N-j} (negative lag). Our grad_j = 2 * c_{(N-j) mod N} from conj(fw)*fa. Alternatively use ifft(fa*conj(fw)) which gives Σ_m a_m w_{m+p}. Need p=-j -> c_{N-j}. Let's test small example w length3 [0,1,0] centered, a [a0,a1]. Desired grad [2a1,2a0]. Let's compute ifft(conj(fw)*fa) c_p=Σ_m w_m a_{m+p} (a out of zero for p negative? In zero-padded length N, for p=1: m=1 w1 a2(0)? a index m+p: m=1 ->a2=0, m? Let's list: w0=0,w1=1,w2=0. c_0=0*a0+1*a1+0*a2= a1. c_1=0*a1+1*a2+0=0. c_{N-1}=m+p=N-1: for m=1? p=N-1 => m+p=N mod N -> a0? c_{N-1}=w1*a0=a0. Good desired grad0=c0, grad1=c_{N-1}. So need take wrap. Our previous simple `[:n]` gives c0,c1, not correct for j>0. Need fix.

Thus implement gradient as:
```
c = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fw)*fa, n=N)  # length N, c[p]=Σ_m w_m a_{m+p mod N}
# For j=0..n-1, grad_j = 2 * c[(N-j) % N]
g = 2.0 * np.concatenate((c[-1:][::-1]?))
```
Let's derive indexing: Desired g_j = 2 * c_{(N-j) mod N}. For j=0 ->c0; j=1 ->c_{N-1}; ... j=n-1 -> c_{N-n+1}. Since N≥3n-2, these indices are in second half. So we can construct:

g = np.empty(n)
for j in range(n): g[j]=c[(N-j)%N]
```
But loop O(n) okay. Vectorized:

Need c maybe circular but N large no overlap.
Alternatively use `ifft(fa * np.conj(fw))` gives d_p=Σ_m a_m w_{m+p}? For real same as c? Let's test: ifft(fa*conj(fw)) = Σ_m a_m w_{m+p}. p=-j desired -> same c_neg. Still need indexing.
Let's test with `ifft(fa*fw)` (convolution) maybe: conv_l=Σ_m a_m w_{l-m}. Desired w_{j+m} a_m =Σ_m a_m w_{(j+m)} => l? If l=j? w_{l+m}? Not. Correlation with reversed weight? Let's use direct formula: Let wr = w[::-1]? Convolution a * wr: (a*wr)_l =Σ_i a_i wr_{l-i}=Σ_i a_i w_{L-1-(l-i)} =Σ_i a_i w_{L-1-l+i}. Set L-1-l+i = j+i => L-1-l=j. So l=L-1-j. Then (a*wr)_ {L-1-j} =Σ_i a_i w_{j+i}. This matches grad_j/2! Great. So we can compute linear convolution of a and reversed weights using FFT:
```
wr = w[::-1]
c = np.fft.ifft(fa*fwr)[:2n-1] maybe length L
g_j = 2*c[L-1-j]
```
Where L=2n-1, wr length L. Convolution length L+n-1=3n-2. The relevant coefficient index L-1-j (between n-1 and 2n-2). This is simpler and avoids circular indexing? We still need one FFT of reversed w. Let's verify small: w [0,1,0], wr [0,1,0] symmetric. conv a*wr: coefficients l: l0 a0*w0=0; l1 a0*w1+a1*w0=a0; l2 a1*w1=a1; l3... Desired j0: L-1=2 c2=a1 yes; j1: L-2=1 c1=a0. Good. So use this. For general w, wr=w[::-1]; conv = ifft(FFT(a, N)*FFT(wr,N)); then g_j=2*conv[L-1-j]. This is correct.
Implementation:
```
def _grad_weighted(w,a):
    n=int(a.size); L=2*n-1
    wr = w[::-1]
    N=_pow2(L+n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wr,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,n=N)
    idx = L-1 - np.arange(n)  # n-1 ... 0? For j=0 ->L-1, j=n-1 ->0
    return 2.0*c[idx]
```
Let's test small j0 L=3 idx2 -> as above. Good. This computes linear convolution length at least L? Convolution of a length n and wr length L has length L+n-1. c[idx] from 0..L-1, valid.

Let's draft code mentally:
```
"""
Uses an analytic one-sided-square-root plus linear-smooth family...
"""
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

def _fft_square(a):
    n=int(a.size)
    L=2*n-1
    N=_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=N).real[:L]
    if b.size < L: b=np.pad(b? but no)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return b
```
For N exactly power >=L, irfft returns length N; [:L] okay.

`_internal_score`:
```
def _internal_score(a,b=None):
    if b is None: b=_fft_square(a)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-300: return float('inf'), b, 0.0
    maxb=float(b.max())
    return float(2.0*a.size*maxb/(s*s)), b, maxb
```

Weights:
```
def _softmax_weights(b, focus, eps=1e-5): ...
def _top_weights(b, k):
    L=b.size; k=int(k); k=max(1,min(L,k)); maxb=b.max()
    w=np.zeros(L)
    if k>=L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
    if maxb>1e-300 and np.isfinite(maxb):
       w[idx]=0.1+0.9*(b[idx]/maxb)
    else: w[idx]=1.0
    s=w.sum()
    if s>0: w/=s
    return w,maxb
```
If b has inf? no.

Gradient:

def _grad_weighted(w,a):
    n=int(a.size); L=int(w.size)
    wr = w[::-1]
    N=_pow2(L+n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wr,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,n=N)
    idx = L-1 - np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    return 2.0*c[idx]
```
Need ensure `idx` nonnegative: L-1-(n-1)=n-1? Wait L=2n-1, L-1=2n-2. idx for j=0 ->2n-2; j=n-1 -> n-1. Convolution length L+n-1=3n-2, max index? 2n-2 <= 3n-3, valid. It uses middle/right side. Good. Let's test with n=1: L=1, idx=0; wr length1. Good.

Param:
```
def _make_density(n, alpha=0.5, beta=7.0, gamma=1.0, reverse=False):
    n=int(n)
    if n<2: return np.ones(max(1,n))/max(1,n)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    if alpha==0:
       base=np.ones(n)
    else:
       base=np.power(x, -float(alpha))
    if beta>1e-14:
       if gamma==0:
          smooth=np.ones(n)
       elif gamma==1:
          smooth=1.0-x
       else:
          smooth=np.power(1.0-x, float(gamma))
       f=base + float(beta)*smooth
    else:
       f=base
    f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
    if reverse:
       f=f[::-1]
    s=f.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    return f/s
```
`reverse` gives same score, but okay.

def _search_param_grid(n, deadline, rng=None):
    alphas=(0.35,0.45,0.5,0.6,0.75,1.0)
    gammas=(0.5,0.75,1.0,1.25,1.5,2.0,3.0)
    betas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,4.0,6.0,8.0,11.0,15.0,22.0,32.0,48.0,72.0,108.0)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf; best_params=None
    # uniform
    p=np.ones(n)/n; sc,_,_=_internal_score(p); update
    # pure base
    for alpha in alphas:
       base=np.power(x,-alpha)
       p=base/base.sum(); sc...; update params dict
       for gamma in gammas:
          smooth=...
          for beta in betas:
             if time>deadline: return
             if beta<=0 continue
             f=base+beta*smooth
             p=f/f.sum(); sc... update
    # pure smooth
    for gamma in gammas:
       smooth...; p...; update
    return best_p,best_score,best_params
```

Local optimizer:

def _optimize_active(a0, tlimit, rng, label=''):
    a=_clean_sequence(a0)
    n=a.size
    b=_fft_square(a); score,b=maxb=_internal...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=0.15; focus=10.0; it=0
    no_accept=0; resets=0; max_no=400
    K=max(256, min(4096, n//16))
    t0=time.monotonic()
    print start
    while time.monotonic() < tlimit:
       # if need b recompute? keep
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_accept=0
       # if time check
       # weights
       if focus < 300:
          w0,_=_softmax_weights(b,focus)
          w1,_=_top_weights(b,K)
          w=0.5*w0+0.5*w1
          focus=min(focus*1.2,2000)
       else:
          w,_=_top_weights(b,K)
       old_wobj=float(np.dot(w,b))
       g=_grad_weighted(w,a)
       g = g - float(np.dot(a,g))
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if scale<1e-18: break
       g /= scale
       trial_a=None; trial_b=None; trial_score=score; trial_eta=0
       # line search
       for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
           e=eta*mult
           # if e too large? log
           logp=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - e*g
           m=logp.max()
           if not np.isfinite(m): break
           logp=logp-m
           an=np.exp(logp)
           an=np.maximum(an,0.0)
           ss=float(an.sum())
           if ss<1e-100: continue
           an/=ss
           an=np.maximum(an,1e-12)
           an/=an.sum()
           bn=_fft_square(an)
           sc,_,_=_internal_score(an,bn)
           nwobj=float(np.dot(w,bn))
           if sc < trial_score - 1e-13:
               trial_score=sc; trial_a=an; trial_b=bn; trial_eta=e; trial_nwobj=nwobj
           elif sc < score*1.002:
               # choose if wobj lower? but only if better than current trial?
               if trial_a is None or nwobj < old_wobj - 1e-12:
                   # Need store only if current score acceptable? we can store even
                   if trial_a is None or sc < trial_score -1e-13:
                       store
       accept=False
       if trial_a is not None:
           if trial_score < score -1e-12:
               accept=True
           else:
               # maybe if weighted obj improves and score not too high
               if trial_score < score*1.002 and float(trial_nwobj) < old_wobj -1e-12:
                   accept=True
       if accept:
           a=trial_a; b=trial_b; score=trial_score; eta=min(eta*1.15,2.0)
           # if best update
       else:
           eta=max(eta*0.7,0.01)
           no_accept +=1
           if no_accept > max_no:
               resets +=1
               if resets > 25: break
               no_accept=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.3)
               fresh = _make_density(n, alpha=float(rng.choice([0.4,0.5,0.6])), beta=float(rng.choice([2,5,7,10,15])), gamma=float(rng.choice([0.75,1.0,1.25])))
               a = 0.8*best_a + 0.2*fresh
               a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=a.sum()
               b=_fft_square(a); score,_,_=_internal_score(a,b)
               eta=0.15; focus=10
       it +=1
       if verbose and it%500==0 print
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _clean_sequence(a, fallback_n=8192):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: arr=_fallback_sequence(fallback_n)
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<=0.01:
        arr=_fallback_sequence(max(arr.size,1024))
        s=arr.sum()
    else:
        arr=arr/s
    return arr
```
Normalize by sum; scaling invariant. But if original sum huge, arr/s small. Good. If there are values 1000, cap.

```
def _base_masses(n, alpha, x=None, i=None):
    n=int(n)
    if alpha == 0:
       return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    if alpha == 1:
       # avoid log singular? use previous midpoint for alpha=0.99? We'll not use alpha=1.
    a = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    b = (a + 1.0) / n? Actually a_i=i/n, b_i=(i+1)/n.
    one_minus_alpha = 1.0 - float(alpha)
    if one_minus_alpha > 1e-12:
        # b^p - a^p; for i=0, 0**p =0 if p>0.
        term_b = np.power(b, one_minus_alpha)
        term_a = np.power(a, one_minus_alpha)  # 0^p=0
        return (term_b - term_a)/one_minus_alpha
    else:
        # alpha close1: use endpoint log approximation? or midpoint
```
For alpha<=1. We'll exclude alpha=1. For alpha=0, formula with p=1 works: (b-a)/1=1/n, okay; we can use general. But 0^1=0. Fine. So if alpha<1.0 (grid max0.9). If alpha<0? no.

Smooth masses:
```
def _smooth_masses(n, gamma):
   g=float(gamma)+1.0
   left = np.arange(n, dtype=float)/n
   right = left+1.0/n
   # y_start = 1-right? Wait bin x from left to right; y=1-x from 1-left down to 1-right.
   y_hi = 1.0 - left
   y_lo = 1.0 - right
   # integral (1/(g)) * (y_hi^g - y_lo^g)
   if g == 1: return (y_hi - y_lo) # =1/n
   else: return (np.power(y_hi,g) - np.power(y_lo,g)) / g
```
For i=n-1, y_lo=0, okay. For gamma=0,g=1 returns 1/n.

```
def _search_param_grid(n, deadline):
   best_p=np.ones(n)/n; best_score=2.0
   alphas=(0.35,0.45,0.5,0.6,0.75,0.9)
   gammas=(0.5,0.75,1.0,1.25,1.5,2.0,3.0)
   betas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1,1.5,2,3,4,6,7,8,11,15,22,32,48,72,108,162,243)
   pure_best? 
   for alpha in alphas:
      base=_base_masses(n,alpha)
      # pure base
      p=base/base.sum(); sc,_,_...
      if sc<best: update, params {'type':'base','alpha':alpha}
      for gamma in gammas:
         smooth=_smooth_masses(n,gamma)
         for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic()>deadline: return
            m=base+beta*smooth
            p=m/m.sum(); sc...
            if sc<best: update params {'type':'mix','alpha':alpha,'beta':beta,'gamma':gamma}
   # pure smooth
   for gamma:
      smooth; p=smooth/smooth.sum(); sc...
   return best_p,best_score,best_params
```

def _refine_params(n, params, deadline, current_p, current_score):
   if params is None: return
   if params.get('type')!='mix': return
   a0=params['alpha']; b0=params['beta']; g0=params['gamma']
   best_p=current_p; best_score=current_score
   alphas_near=(a0-0.05,a0,a0+0.05)
   betas_near=(b0*0.6,b0*0.8,b0,b0*1.2,b0*1.6)
   gammas_near=(g0-0.15,g0,g0+0.15)
   for a in alphas_near:
      if a<=0 or a>=1: continue
      base=_base_masses(n,a)
      for g in gammas_near:
         if g<0.05: continue
         smooth=_smooth_masses(n,g)
         for b in betas_near:
            if b<1e-6: continue
            m=base+b*smooth; p=m/m.sum(); sc...
            if sc<best: update
            if time>deadline return
```

def _base_masses(n, alpha):
    n=int(n)
    if n <=0: return np.array([0.0])
    a=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
    b=a+1.0/float(n)
    alpha=float(alpha)
    if alpha < 1e-8:
       return np.full(n, 1.0/float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    p=1.0-alpha
    if p > 1e-8:
       # for a=0, np.power(0,p) is 0
       return (np.power(b,p) - np.power(a,p))/p
    else:
       # alpha close to 1: use log integral
       # ∫ x^-1 dx = log(b)-log(a), first bin log(1/n)-log(1e?) maybe set using midpoint for small?
       # Use simple midpoint h = a^{-alpha} * dx but for a=0 set n^{-alpha}/sqrt? Use mass from alpha=0.999.
       a_safe=np.maximum(a, 0.5/float(n))
       return a_safe**(-alpha)/float(n)
```

def _smooth_masses(n,gamma):
    n=int(n); left=np.arange(n)/n; right=left+1/n
    g=gamma+1.0
    y_hi=1-left; y_lo=1-right
    if abs(g-1)<1e-12: return np.full(n,1/n)
    return (np.power(y_hi,g)-np.power(y_lo,g))/g

```python
"""
Optimizer for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing

    2 * n * max_k (a * a)[k] / (sum a)**2.

The main construction uses an analytic family of bin probabilities

    p_i ∝ ∫_{i/n}^{(i+1)/n} [ x**(-alpha) + beta * (1-x)**gamma ] dx,

which is evaluated by exact bin integrals.  This family is known to
go well below the uniform sequence: the alpha=0.5, gamma=1, beta≈7
member has asymptotic score near 1.37.  A fast FFT evaluator is used
to scan/refine the family, and then a guarded mirror-descent /
subgradient loop is applied to polish the vector.

The program keeps a guaranteed-good fallback candidate and always
returns a valid [0, 1000] sequence with sum > 0.01.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_square(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _internal_score(a, b=None):
    if b is None:
        b = _fft_square(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf"), b, 0.0
    maxb = float(b.max())
    score = float(2.0 * a.size * maxb / (s * s))
    return score, b, maxb


def _softmax_weights(b, focus, eps=1e-5):
    L = int(b.size)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300 or L <= 0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        z = (maxb - b) / maxb
        p = np.exp(-z * float(focus))

    s = float(p.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-12:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = (1.0 - eps) * (p / s) + eps / L
    return p, maxb


def _top_weights(b, k):
    L = int(b.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    k = int(k)
    k = max(1, min(L, k))
    maxb = float(b.max())
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

    if k >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]

    if maxb > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(maxb):
        w[idx] = 0.1 + 0.9 * (b[idx] / maxb)
    else:
        w[idx] = 1.0

    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        w = w / s
    return w, maxb


def _grad_weighted(w, a):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (a*a)[k] with respect to a_j.

    If wr = w[::-1], then (a * wr)[L-1-j] = sum_i a_i w_{j+i},
    and the gradient is 2 times that.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)
    wr = w[::-1]
    N = _pow2(L + n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, n=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=N)
    idx = (L - 1) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    return 2.0 * c[idx]


def _base_masses(n, alpha):
    """Bin masses for x**(-alpha) on [0, 1]."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    b = a + 1.0 / float(n)
    alpha = float(alpha)

    if alpha < 1e-8:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    p = 1.0 - alpha
    if p > 1e-8:
        return (np.power(b, p) - np.power(a, p)) / p

    # Fallback for alpha extremely close to 1 (not used in the main scan).
    a_safe = np.maximum(a, 0.5 / float(n))
    return np.power(a_safe, -alpha) / float(n)


def _smooth_masses(n, gamma):
    """Bin masses for (1-x)**gamma on [0, 1]."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    left = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    right = left + 1.0 / float(n)
    g = float(gamma) + 1.0

    y_hi = 1.0 - left
    y_lo = 1.0 - right

    if abs(g - 1.0) < 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    return (np.power(y_hi, g) - np.power(y_lo, g)) / g


def _mass_from_parts(base, smooth, beta, reverse=False):
    if beta > 1e-14 and smooth is not None:
        m = base + float(beta) * smooth
    else:
        m = base.copy()

    m = np.nan_to_num(m, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = np.maximum(m, 0.0)

    if reverse:
        m = m[::-1]

    s = float(m.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, m.size)

    return m / s


def _make_density(n, alpha, beta, gamma, reverse=False):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    base = _base_masses(n, alpha)
    smooth = None
    if beta > 1e-14:
        smooth = _smooth_masses(n, gamma)

    return _mass_from_parts(base, smooth, beta, reverse=reverse)


def _fallback_sequence(n=8192):
    # This one-sided square-root + linear-smooth member is already strong.
    return _make_density(int(n), alpha=0.5, beta=7.0, gamma=1.0)


def _clean_sequence(a, fallback_n=8192):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size <= 0:
        arr = _fallback_sequence(int(fallback_n))

    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())

    if s <= 0.01:
        arr = _fallback_sequence(max(int(arr.size), 1024))
        s = float(arr.sum())
    else:
        arr = arr / s

    return arr


def _score_param(n, alpha, beta, gamma, reverse=False):
    p = _make_density(n, alpha, beta, gamma, reverse=reverse)
    b = _fft_square(p)
    sc, _, _ = _internal_score(p, b)
    return sc, p, b


def _search_param_grid(n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    best_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best_score = 2.0
    best_params = None

    alphas = (0.35, 0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9)
    gammas = (0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0)
    betas = (
        0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0,
        6.0, 7.0, 8.0, 11.0, 15.0, 22.0, 32.0, 48.0, 72.0,
        108.0, 162.0, 243.0
    )

    # Uniform is already best_p.

    for alpha in alphas:
        base = _base_masses(n, alpha)
        base_s = float(base.sum())
        if base_s > 1e-300:
            p = base / base_s
            b = _fft_square(p)
            sc, _, _ = _internal_score(p, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p
                best_params = {"type": "base", "alpha": float(alpha)}

        for gamma in gammas:
            smooth = _smooth_masses(n, gamma)
            for beta in betas:
                if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                    return best_p, best_score, best_params

                m = base + float(beta) * smooth
                ms = float(m.sum())
                if ms <= 1e-300:
                    continue

                p = m / ms
                b = _fft_square(p)
                sc, _, _ = _internal_score(p, b)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_p = p
                    best_params = {
                        "type": "mix",
                        "alpha": float(alpha),
                        "beta": float(beta),
                        "gamma": float(gamma),
                    }

    # Pure smooth-only sequences, for completeness.
    for gamma in gammas:
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break
        smooth = _smooth_masses(n, gamma)
        ss = float(smooth.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-300:
            continue
        p = smooth / ss
        b = _fft_square(p)
        sc, _, _ = _internal_score(p, b)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p
            best_params = {"type": "smooth", "gamma": float(gamma)}

    return best_p, best_score, best_params


def _refine_params(n, params, deadline, current_p, current_score):
    if params is None or params.get("type") != "mix":
        # If no mix parameters were found, try a small set of robust defaults.
        candidates = (
            (0.5, 6.0, 1.0),
            (0.5, 7.0, 1.0),
            (0.5, 8.0, 1.0),
            (0.55, 7.0, 1.0),
            (0.45, 7.0, 1.0),
        )
    else:
        a0 = float(params.get("alpha", 0.5))
        b0 = float(params.get("beta", 7.0))
        g0 = float(params.get("gamma", 1.0))

        candidates = []
        for da in (-0.05, 0.0, 0.05):
            a = a0 + da
            if a <= 1e-3 or a >= 0.995:
                continue
            for bg in (0.6, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.3, 1.6):
                b = b0 * bg
                if b <= 0.0:
                    continue
                for dd in (-0.2, -0.1, 0.0, 0.1, 0.2):
                    g = g0 + dd
                    if g < 0.05:
                        g = 0.05
                    candidates.append((float(a), float(b), float(g)))

    best_p = np.asarray(current_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_score = float(current_score)

    for (a, b, g) in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break
        try:
            sc, p, _ = _score_param(n, a, b, g)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p

    return best_p, best_score


def _optimize_active(a0, deadline, rng, verbose=False, label="main"):
    a = _clean_sequence(a0)
    n = int(a.size)

    b = _fft_square(a)
    score, _, _ = _internal_score(a, b)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        fb = _fallback_sequence(n)
        b = _fft_square(fb)
        best_a = fb.copy()
        score, _, _ = _internal_score(fb, b)
        best_score = score
        a = fb.copy()

    eta = 0.18
    focus = 10.0
    it = 0
    no_accept = 0
    max_no = 500
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 25
    K = max(256, min(4096, n // 16))

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        if verbose and (it % 1000 == 0):
            print(
                f"[{label}] local it={it} t={time.monotonic():.0f}s "
                f"score={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} "
                f"focus={focus:.2f} eta={eta:.4f} resets={resets}"
            )

        if not np.isfinite(score):
            a = best_a.copy()
            b = _fft_square(a)
            score, _, _ = _internal_score(a, b)
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                break

        # Mixed weights: soft focus plus an active-top component.
        if focus < 300.0:
            w0, _ = _softmax_weights(b, focus)
            w1, _ = _top_weights(b, K)
            w = 0.5 * w0 + 0.5 * w1
            focus = min(focus * 1.25, 2000.0)
        else:
            w, _ = _top_weights(b, K)

        old_wobj = float(np.dot(w, b))

        try:
            g = _grad_weighted(w, a)
        except Exception:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        # Constants vanish after normalization; center under current p.
        g = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-18:
            break
        g = g / scale

        trial_a = None
        trial_b = None
        trial_score = score
        trial_wobj = old_wobj
        accept = False

        for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            e = eta * mult
            try:
                lp = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) - e * g
                m = float(np.max(lp))
                if not np.isfinite(m):
                    continue
                lp = lp - m
                an = np.exp(lp)
                an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
                ss = float(an.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-100:
                    continue
                an = an / ss
                an = np.maximum(an, 1e-12)
                an = an / float(an.sum())

                bn = _fft_square(an)
                sc, _, _ = _internal_score(an, bn)
                nwobj = float(np.dot(w, bn))

                good_strict = (np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-13)
                good_soft = (
                    np.isfinite(sc)
                    and sc < score * 1.002
                    and nwobj < old_wobj - 1e-12
                )

                if good_strict or good_soft:
                    if (
                        trial_a is None
                        or sc < trial_score - 1e-13
                        or (abs(sc - trial_score) <= 1e-13 and nwobj < trial_wobj)
                    ):
                        trial_a = an
                        trial_b = bn
                        trial_score = sc
                        trial_wobj = nwobj
            except Exception:
                continue

        if trial_a is not None:
            if trial_score < score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif trial_score < score * 1.002 and trial_wobj < old_wobj - 1e-12:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            a = trial_a
            b = trial_b
            score = trial_score
            eta = min(eta * 1.15, 2.5)
            no_accept = 0
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.72, 0.01)
            no_accept += 1

            if no_accept > max_no:
                resets += 1
                if resets > max_resets:
                    break
                no_accept = 0
                max_no = int(max_no * 1.35)

                alphas = (0.45, 0.5, 0.55)
                betas = (4.0, 6.0, 7.0, 9.0, 12.0)
                gammas = (0.75, 1.0, 1.25)
                fresh = _make_density(
                    n,
                    alpha=float(rng.choice(alphas)),
                    beta=float(rng.choice(betas)),
                    gamma=float(rng.choice(gammas)),
                )
                a = 0.85 * best_a + 0.15 * fresh
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
                a = a / float(a.sum())
                b = _fft_square(a)
                score, _, _ = _internal_score(a, b)
                eta = 0.18
                focus = 10.0

        it += 1

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _safe_evaluate(seq):
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if ev is None or not callable(ev):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        return float(ev([float(x) for x in seq]))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0, **kwargs):
    # Allow a few common budget argument names.
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2350.0

    for key in ("budget", "budget_seconds", "time", "time_limit", "max_time"):
        if key in kwargs:
            try:
                budget = float(kwargs[key])
                break
            except Exception:
                pass

    budget = max(120.0, min(budget, 2400.0))
    start0 = time.monotonic()
    reserve = 90.0
    deadline = start0 + max(120.0, budget - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**32))
    n_main = 8192

    print(
        f"starting improved step-function optimizer "
        f"budget={budget:.1f}s deadline_offset={deadline - start0:.1f}s"
    )

    best_p = _fallback_sequence(n_main)
    best_score = _internal_score(best_p)[0]

    # Optionally use the previously-known sequence if it is already good.
    given = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if given is not None and len(given) > 0:
        try:
            g = _clean_sequence(given, fallback_n=n_main)
            gs, _, _ = _internal_score(g)
            print(f"given internal score={gs:.6f} n={g.size}")
            if np.isfinite(gs) and gs < best_score:
                best_p = g
                best_score = gs
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Stage 1: fast analytic family scan at final length.
    param_deadline = min(deadline, start0 + 420.0)
    print("parametric scan start")
    try:
        p2, s2, params = _search_param_grid(n_main, param_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_p = p2
            best_score = s2
        else:
            p2 = _fallback_sequence(n_main)
            s2 = _internal_score(p2)[0]
        print(f"parametric scan score={s2:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("parametric scan failed:", repr(exc))
        p2 = _fallback_sequence(n_main)
        s2 = _internal_score(p2)[0]
        params = None

    if params is None:
        # If we lost parameters, set a robust default nearby.
        params = {"type": "mix", "alpha": 0.5, "beta": 7.0, "gamma": 1.0}

    # Stage 2: refine the analytic parameters.
    refine_deadline = min(deadline, max(param_deadline + 10.0, start0 + 600.0))
    print("parameter refinement start")
    try:
        p3, s3 = _refine_params(
            n_main, params, refine_deadline, best_p, best_score
        )
        if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
            best_p = p3
            best_score = s3
        print(f"parameter refinement best={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("parameter refinement failed:", repr(exc))

    # Stage 3: polishing by guarded multiplicative gradient descent.
    local_deadline = max(start0 + 60.0, deadline - 30.0)
    remaining = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 180.0:
        print("local polishing start")
        try:
            p4, s4 = _optimize_active(
                best_p, local_deadline, rng, verbose=True, label="main"
            )
            if np.isfinite(s4) and s4 < best_score:
                best_p = p4
                best_score = s4
            print(f"local polishing best={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("local polishing failed:", repr(exc))
    else:
        print("skipping local polishing (not enough time)")

    # Final cleanup.
    best_p = _clean_sequence(best_p, fallback_n=n_main)
    final_internal, _, _ = _internal_score(best_p)
    print(f"final internal score={final_internal:.6f} n={best_p.size}")

    # If time is available and length is moderate, sanity-check with the
    # evaluator.  This is not required, but helps catch unexpected issues.
    remaining = (start0 + float(budget)) - time.monotonic()
    if best_p.size <= 12000 and remaining > 25.0:
        try:
            exact = _safe_evaluate(best_p)
            if np.isfinite(exact):
                print(f"final exact score={exact:.6f}")
            else:
                print("final exact unavailable")
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```
